HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 56, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(56, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(14, 56, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(56, 56, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(56, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )

        (conv3): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)

        (conv_f): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (wavelet_attn): WaveletAttention(
        (block): WaveletTransformerBlock(
          (norm1): LN2d(
            (n): LayerNorm((224,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
          )
          (attn): WaveletWindowAttention(
            (qkv): Linear(in_features=224, out_features=672, bias=True)
            (proj): Linear(in_features=224, out_features=224, bias=True)
          )
          (norm2): LN2d(
            (n): LayerNorm((224,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
          )
          (ffn): ConvFFN(
            (pw1): Conv2d(224, 224, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (dw): Conv2d(224, 224, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=224)
            (pw2): Conv2d(224, 224, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (act): GELU(approximate='none')
          )
        )
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(56, 56, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(56, 14, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(14, 14, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

      )

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )

      )
    )

  (LR_conv2): Conv2d(56, 56, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (wavelet_attn): WaveletAttention(
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x WaveletTransformerBlock(
            (norm1): LN2d(
              (n): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (attn): WaveletWindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=1536, bias=True)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=512, out_features=512, bias=True)
            )
            (norm2): LN2d(
              (n): LayerNorm((512,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            )
            (ffn): ConvFFN(
              (pw1): Conv2d(512, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (dw): Conv2d(512, 512, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=512)
              (pw2): Conv2d(512, 512, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
              (act): GELU(approximate='none')
            )
          )
        )
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(

        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
  )
  (LR_conv1): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (LR_conv2): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (pixel_shuffle): PixelShuffle(upscale_factor=4)
  (recon_conv1): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
  (recon_conv2): Conv2d(128, 48, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
)
Model(
  (model): HUTCN(
    (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (post_unet_esa): ESA(
      (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
      (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
      (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
    )
